- **cis-regulatory-motif-enrichment-filtering**: Use when converting co-expression modules into directed gene regulatory networks: after GRNBoost2 proposes candidate TF–TG edges from single-cell expression data, to retain only edges with cis-regulatory motif evidence. - **cis-trans-gene-signature-annotation**: Use when significant cis/trans gene sets from iEDGE differential expression and mediation analysis need functional annotation, e.g. detecting hallmark pathways recurrently enriched across multiple SCNAs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/cis-trans-gene-signature-annotation))
- **cis-trans-mediation-ranking**: Use when a cis/trans differential-expression signature table exists for SCNA events and candidate cis genes must be ranked by their trans mediation to designate the Rank-1 driver per alteration. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/cis-trans-mediation-ranking))
